## 写作约束
- **7-gen vs 6-gen 数字禁止直接对比** (2026-05-15 Director 硬约束): 7-gen binary F1=0.768 不可与 6-gen binary F1=0.723 做 apple-to-apple 比较。原因：训练集组成变化（多了 instruct 数据），提升主要来自 training diversity。写 instruct scope limitation 时只报告 instruct held-out 绝对 F1 和同架构 Δ，不引用 aggregate 对比。
- **Instruct 方向反转是长度伪影** (2026-05-15 Reviewer CRITICAL, 已验证): instruct 模型输出极短（0.6B depth-1 仅 35 tokens vs depth-0 252 tokens），length-depth ρ > 0.5。Length-matched 后 surface feature ρ 降至 ~0（0.6B）或缩减 53-91%（1.7B），entropy 同理。**不可 claim 方向反转**。Discussion 用纯 "method boundary" 叙事：instruct 链不产生可检测的 depth signal（gen-1 vs gen-2+ F1 ≈ 0.5）。

## 可查的历史
前 58 轮 handoff 在 `task_context/history/agent/loop_*.md`，如有疑问可 Read 回查。

    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：诊断两个死进程并更新 registry\n\n服务器：autodl-5090\n工作目录：/root/gen-depth-fingerprint\nPython 环境：conda base (`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`)\n\n### 实验 1: plan-006-confound-fix\n- PID 256127（已死），cuda:1\n- 日志：/tmp/plan006_confound_fix.log\n- 假设：paraphrastic chains confound 修复（rp=1.0, max_tokens=256 matched params）\n- **目标**：读日志判断是完成还是崩溃\n  - 如果完成：找结果文件（可能在 results/ 或 /tmp/ 或日志末尾），提取 metrics（3-class F1, binary F1, paraphrastic vs continuation delta）\n  - 如果崩溃：记录错误信息和崩溃位置\n\n### 实验 2: plan-012-qwen06b-v2\n- 进程已死，cuda:2\n- 结果文件：检查 /root/gen-depth-fingerprint/results/plan012_qwen06b_seed123_r32_results.json\n- 假设：Qwen3-0.6B-Base QLoRA 超参敏感性（seed=123, LoRA r=32 vs 原版 seed=42, r=16）\n- 原版对比：plan-012-qwen06b 已成功，5-class F1=0.5828, 3-class F1=0.9052\n- **目标**：\n  - 如果结果文件存在：提取 metrics，与原版对比计算 delta\n  - 如果不存在：查日志（可能在 logs/ 或 /tmp/），判断崩溃原因\n\n### 产出要求\n1. 对每个实验：结论是 success/failed/incomplete + 具体 metrics 或错误信息\n2. 用 `update_registry` 更新两个实验状态：\n   - 如果完成：status=DONE, conclusion=success/negative, 写入 metrics\n   - 如果崩溃：status=DONE, conclusion=failed, notes 写入错误原因\n3. 最后输出一段文字总结两个实验的诊断结果，方便 Agent 汇报 Director"

  "task_prompt": "## 任务：将 P1-E ordinal regression 结论写入论文\n\n论文文件路径：/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0514_3/projects/gen-depth-fingerprint/artifacts/_project/paper/\n\n### 实验结果（来自 registry review-p1e-ordinal + plan-009-finer-ordinal）\n\n**3-class ordinal comparison (review-p1e-ordinal)**:\n- XGBoost 3-class F1 = 0.789, binary F1 = 0.721\n- CORAL 3-class F1 = 0.771, binary F1 = 0.728\n- Frank-Hall 3-class F1 = 0.793\n- Concordance: XGBoost 0.821, CORAL 0.810, Frank-Hall 0.825\n- **结论**: CI 重叠，ordinal methods 无显著优势（negligible benefit）\n\n**5-class finer ordinal (plan-009-finer-ordinal)**:\n- 5-class HGBT macro F1 = 0.503, accuracy = 0.542, MAE = 0.659\n- 5-class 最好 = HGBT (0.503), RF (0.477), LR (0.447), Frank-Hall (0.451)\n- Per-class F1: depth_0=0.913, depth_1=0.628, depth_2=0.269, depth_3=0.265, depth_4=0.439\n- gen2/gen3 不可分（inseparable）\n- 3-class HGBT F1 = 0.782\n- **结论**: negative — 5-class 分类不可行，gen2 和 gen3 无法区分\n\n### 写入位置\n1. **results.tex**: 在 ablation 或 additional analysis 部分添加 1-2 段 ordinal comparison 结论\n   - 3-class: CORAL/Frank-Hall 与 XGBoost CI 重叠，ordinal structure 无显著增益\n   - 5-class: macro F1 从 0.789 降至 0.503，gen-2/gen-3 confusion 是主因\n2. **appendix_finer_depth.tex**: 检查是否已有 5-class 详细结果，如无则添加完整表格\n3. **discussion.tex**: 如有 ordinal 相关讨论，确保与结论一致\n\n### 写作约束\n- 不改 framing，只添加 ordinal comparison 结论\n- 用 \"negligible\" / \"no significant advantage\" 描述 3-class ordinal，不要 overstate\n- 5-class negative 结果用 \"boundary condition\" 叙事：gen-2 和 gen-3 在特征空间中不可分，是方法的自然边界\n- 数字精度：F1 保留 3 位小数，CI 用 [lower, upper] 格式\n- 每个定量 claim 附 exp_id（如 \"plan-009-finer-ordinal\" 或 \"review-p1e-ordinal\"）作为注释\n\n### 验证\n修改完后列出所有新增/修改段落的位置和内容摘要。"
